Gradient:
```
def _lse_grad(x):
    a = _softmax(x)
    A = rfft(a,L)
    c = irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
    cmax = c.max()
    w = exp((c-cmax)/tau); w/=w.sum()
    W = rfft(w,L)
    h = irfft(np.conj(A)*W, L)[:n]
    g_a = 2.0*h
    dot = np.dot(a, g_a)
    g_x = a*(g_a - dot)
    return g_x
```

**Correct LSE gradient proof and implementation**:
For full convolution c_k=∑_i a_i a_{k-i}. derivative wrt a_j: 2∑_r a_r w_{j+r}. We computed via FFT h=ifft(conj(A)*W)[:n] gives h_j=∑ a_r w_{j+r}? Let's verify with direct convolution formula.
Let W=FFT(w,L) (zero padded). A=FFT(a,L). `ifft(conj(A)*W)[j] = ∑_{m=0}^{L-1} A_m^* W_{m+j mod L}` (because product and ifft: c_j=∑_m X_m Y_{m+j}). With real zero padded, for j=0..n-1, m from 0..L-1. A_m^* nonzero for m<n; W_{m+j} nonzero if m+j<2n-1? Since L≥2n-1 and no wrap, for m≥n zero. Thus c_j = ∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a_m w_{m+j} = desired. Good. So grad_a = 2*real(ifft(conj(A)*W)[:n]). Need L≥2n-1. Good.

But when computing c = ifft(A*A)[:M], note A*A corresponds to circular convolution of a with itself. Since L≥2n-1, no wrap. Good.

Implementation:
```
_OPT_N=...; _OPT_L=...; _OPT_TAU=...
_CACHE_NAME = None
_CACHE_VAL = None
_CACHE_GRAD = None

def _compute_lse_a(x):
    a=softmax(x)
    A=fft(a,L)
    c=irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
    cmax=c.max()
    if tau<=0: ...
    ex=np.exp((c-cmax)/tau); w=ex/ex.sum()
    val=cmax+tau*np.log(ex.sum()) if tau>0 else cmax
    W=fft(w,L)
    h=irfft(np.conj(A)*W,L)[:n].real
    g_a=2*h
    dot=np.dot(a,g_a)
    g_x=a*(g_a-dot)
    return val,g_x,a,cmax
```

**Resampling to power-of-two lengths**: If we want try n=4096, L=8192. Starting from height 4806. For resampling, we can use interpolation of cumulative distribution? Since sequence is probability, linear interpolation of density after adjusting endpoints maybe. `np.interp(linspace(0,1,n), linspace(0,1,m), a*m?)` If values represent heights, scaling by length to preserve integral? Discrete sum approximates integral; to keep sum 1, linear interpolation of values and renormalize works approximately. We can also use FFT upsampling. Simpler:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    m=len(a)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<1e-12: b=np.ones(n)/n
    else: b/=s
    return b
```
This preserves sum. For n much larger, values smaller after interp? Since a sum=1, interpolation new sum approx old sum * (n/m)? Wait if a are heights over unit interval with discrete sum approximating integral? If old a_i = f(i/(m-1)), sum approx m∫f. To keep sum=1, values scale ~1/m. Interpolating same values gives sum ~ n∫f, larger by n/m. Renormalizing corrects. Good.

If final sequence length changes, score uses n; linearly interpolated density approximates continuous optimum, maybe good.

**Concrete implementation of functions** (top-level, no lambdas):

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import math

_TINY = 1e-15

def _as_positive_normalized(v):
    a=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
       a=np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
       s=1
    else:
       a/=s
    return a
```
But if len 0? Return list? We ensure.

`_fft_length(n)`: return 1 << ((2*n-1).bit_length()) maybe. For n small. Use int.

`_fft_conv(a, L=None)`:
```
if L is None: L=_fft_length(len(a))
F=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)
return c[:2*len(a)-1]
```
Using rfft for real; F*F maybe complex, irfft. Good.

`_score_from_c(c,n)`:

m = float(np.max(c))
if not finite: inf
return 2.0*n*m  # since sum=1
```

`_exact_score(a)`:
```
n=len(a); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12 return inf; a=a/s; c=_fft_conv(a); return 2*n*float(c.max())
```
Could just call evaluate_sequence for validation. But using FFT in loop.

`_correlate_weights(v, a, L)`:
```
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
V=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*V, L)[:len(a)]
h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)? It should be nonnegative if v,a nonnegative. Round tiny negative set 0.
return h
```

x = x - x.max()

return ex/s
```

`_opt_compute(x)`:
```
n=_OPT_n; L=_OPT_L; tau=_OPT_tau; M=_OPT_M
a=_opt_softmax(x)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
# if tau small infinite? tau>0.
# If tau <= 1e-300 use exact max gradient maybe? We'll ensure >0.
cmax = float(c.max())
# LSE weights stable:
exarg=(c-cmax)/tau
# exarg min could be very negative; exp underflows to 0 okay.
ex=np.exp(exarg)
wsum=ex.sum()
if wsum <=0 or not finite: # fallback uniform active top?
   w=np.zeros(M); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   val=cmax
else:
   w=ex/wsum
   val=cmax + tau*math.log(wsum)
V=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*V,L)[:n]
h=np.maximum(h,0.0) # due roundoff; note weights nonneg, a nonneg, so h nonneg.
g_a=2.0*h
dot=float(np.dot(a,g_a))
g_x=a*(g_a - dot)
# remove tiny nonfinite
g_x=np.nan_to_num(g_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
return val,g_x
```

Objective:
```
def _opt_objective(x):
   global _OPT_cache_x, _OPT_cache_val, _OPT_cache_grad
   val, grad = _opt_compute(x)
   _OPT_cache_x = x.copy()
   _OPT_cache_val = val
   _OPT_cache_grad = grad
   return val
```
Gradient:
```
def _opt_gradient(x):
   global ...
   if _OPT_cache_grad is not None and _OPT_cache_x is not None and _OPT_cache_x.shape == x.shape:
       # If x exactly same? use allclose maybe
       if np.allclose(x, _OPT_cache_x, rtol=0.0, atol=1e-15):
           return _OPT_cache_grad
   val, grad = _opt_compute(x)
   return grad
```
This copies x each objective; if objective called with large x many times, memory okay.

**Entropy update implementation**:

`_entropy_update(a, step, eps=1e-12)`:
- n=len(a); L
- c = _fft_conv(a,L)
- cmax = c.max()
- if cmax <= eps: return a, cmax
- v = 1.0/(c + eps)  # units: c~1/n, v~n. Maybe normalize by median to avoid scale.
- # Flatten v: cap at maybe 20 * median positive. Compute med = np.median(v[v>0]); if med>0: v = np.minimum(v, 100*med)
- h = _correlate_weights(v,a,L)  # positive
- h = np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
- logh = np.log(h); mean = np.mean(logh)
- loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) + step * (logh - mean)
- a_new = softmax(loga)
- return a_new
```
But if step large, okay. We'll accept only if score lower. In loop, try steps [step, step/2, step/4]. Line search limited to 3 evals per iter maybe too many. We can just do one step and if worsens, reduce step. Use current best score.

**Additive projected subgradient refiner**:

def _active_gradient_update(a, weights, step):

def score_fast(a):

      We want encourage low lags. Let normalized gap r=(cmax-c)/cmax ∈[0,1]. Use v = 1 + gamma*r, gamma maybe 5-20. Then h increases where adjacent low lags. This avoids endpoint explosion. For uniform, endpoints c~1/n^2, r≈1, v=1+gamma; middle central r=0, v=1. Good.

   - Since v between 1 and 1+gamma, scale comparable. h varies gently.
   - Update loga with step*(logh - mean(logh)). Step maybe 0.1.
   This is better than 1/c.

Let's implement `entropy_candidate(a, gamma, step, L=None)`.
   - If any a zero, log zero. Use `a_pos = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)`. `loga=np.log(a_pos)`.
   - c = conv_fft(a); cmax; if cmax<=0 return a
   - v = 1 + gamma * (cmax - c)/cmax; cap. `v/=v.mean()` optional.
   - h = _correlate_weights(v,a,L); h positive. If h max <=0: return a.
   - logh = np.log(np.maximum(h, tiny)); center subtract mean.
   - loga_new = loga + step*(logh - mean)
   - b=softmax(loga_new)
   - return b
This update can increase low c neighbors and decrease high. It may be slow but stable. Use line search with steps [step, 0.5 step,0.25]. Update step adapt.

2. `subgradient_refine(a, deadline, tag)`:
   - similar but weights top peaks and additive.
   - Use current c to compute weights: LSE with tau = max(1e-12, gap). We can choose `tau = max(1e-12, 0.1*cmax)`. h = correlate -> g_a=2*h.
   - We need direction that may increase/decrease. Use additive: `delta = step * np.mean(a) / (np.mean(np.abs(g))+eps)`; b=a - delta*g; project and normalize.
   - Try multiples.
   - If no improvement for many, reduce step.
   - Also can use **multiplicative** version: loga -= step * (g_scaled - mean(g_scaled)) with g_scaled = g/(std+eps), step 0.01, project via softmax. Maybe safer positivity. We can implement both and choose best among candidates per iteration (limited).

def make_heuristic(n):
   i = np.arange(n)
   edge = np.minimum(i, n-1-i)
   a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(edge + 1.0)
   # maybe blend with inverse beta:
   b = 1.0 / np.sqrt((i+1)*(n-i))
   c = 0.5*a + 0.5*b
   # add small random? deterministic maybe.
   a = np.maximum(c, 0)
   a /= a.sum()
   return a
```

def entropy_refine(a, deadline, tag, initial_lam=0.1, initial_step=0.2):
  best=a; sc=score_fast
  lam=0.1; step=0.2; stuck=0
  while now<deadline:
     # maybe measure improvement over last; if no improvement 50 iters reduce lam? or increase?
     cand = entropy_candidate(best, lam, step)
     sc_new=score_fast(cand)
     if sc_new < sc:
        accept; sc_best update; step=min(1.0, step*1.1); stuck=0
     else:
        # try half
        cand=... step*0.5; if improve accept; else step*=0.7; stuck++
     # schedule lam: gradually decrease from 0.1 to 0.001 over 500 iter
     lam = max(1e-3, lam*0.99)
     # step floor 0.02; if stuck>50 reset step=0.2, lam=0.01? or break
```

**Detailed `lbfgs_refine` implementation with time**:
```
def lbfgs_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline = start+time_limit
    n=len(a); L=fft_len(n); M=2n-1
    globals set n,L,M
    sc=score_fast(a)
    cmax=sc/(2*n)
    if cmax <=0: return a,sc

def refine_sequence(a, time_limit, tag):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+time_limit
    best=a; best_score=score_fast(a)
    # ensure length enough
    if len(best)<2 or time_limit<=0: return best,bst

    # lbfgs 40%

    if t>10: best,sc=lbfgs_refine(best, t, tag)
    # subgrad 20%

    if t>5: best,sc=subgradient_refine(best, t, tag)
    # second lbfgs 10%
    t = min(deadline - now, time_limit*0.10)
    if t>5: best,sc=lbfgs_refine(best, t, tag)
    # entropy 10%
    ...
    return best,best_score
```

def make_heuristic(n, seed=12345):
    i=np.arange(n)
    edge=np.minimum(i,n-1-i)
    a1=1.0/np.sqrt(edge+1.0)
    a2=1.0/np.sqrt((i+1.0)*(n-i))
    a=(a1+0.5*a2)
    # asymmetric small tilt
    a[a<0]=0
    # random low noise
    rng=np.random.default_rng(2024)
    a = a * (1.0 + 0.05*rng.normal(size=n))
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=...: uniform
    return a/s
```

Let's implement `half_binomial(n)`:
```
def half_binomial(n):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n>0: a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        a[i] = a[i-1] * (2*i - 1)/(2*i)
    s=a.sum()
    return a/s
```
O(n) fine. For n=4806. a values ~1/√(π i). 
`mix_halfbin(a1,a2,alpha)`: a=alpha*a1+(1-alpha)*a2; normalize. Sweep maybe:
```
def heuristic_candidates(n):
   cands=[]
   h=half_binomial(n); candidates h, rev
   for alpha in [0.9,0.8,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2]:
      cands.append(normalize(alpha*h+(1-alpha)*np.roll? reversed rev))
   # U-shaped edge sqrt
   i=np.arange(n); edge=np.minimum(i,n-1-i); c=1/sqrt(edge+1); normalize
   # arcsine
   c=1/sqrt((i+1)*(n-i)); normalize
   # side singular with two endpoints small
   c=1/np.sqrt(i+1) + 0.3/np.sqrt(n-i)
   # random perturb h
   rng noise
```

Let's formalize update:
```
v = 1.0 / (c + eps)
h = correlate(v,a)
a_new = a * exp(step*(logh - mean(logh)))
```
This is what we have with lam = eps/cmax. `eps` can be `lambda * cmax`. Choose lambda small. This is the **Von Neumann' regularized iterative scaling**. We can run it with decaying lambda.

Implement:
```
def entropy_candidate_with_score(a, lam, step, L=None):
   n=len(a); if L None L=fft_len(n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2n-1]
   cmax=c.max()
   if cmax<=0: return a, 2n*cmax
   scale=max(lam*cmax, _TINY)
   v=1.0/(c+scale)
   cap=1.0/scale
   v=np.minimum(v, cap)
   V=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*V,L)[:n]
   h=np.maximum(h, _TINY)
   a_pos=np.maximum(a, _TINY)
   loga=np.log(a_pos)
   logh=np.log(h)
   loga_new=loga + step*(logh - np.mean(logh))
   b=np.exp(loga_new - loga_new.max()); b/=b.sum()
   Ab=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
   cb=np.fft.irfft(Ab*Ab,L)[:2n-1]
   sc=2*n*cb.max()/(b.sum()**2) # sum ~1
   return b, sc
```
Since b sum1, score=2n max. Use sum. If sc nonfinite, inf.

For line search, call with step and 0.5step. Each call recomputes current c and h from same a; if doing two attempts, duplicate current c. We can optimize by precomputing current c and h? - `_entropy_precompute(a, lam, L)` returns c, cmax, scale, v, h. It computes A? Need A for h. Return A too? A rfft then v rfft. We can return A, c, cmax, scale, v, h. Then candidate step uses A? Actually h requires A and V. If step only changes loga update, candidate b differs; h constant. Candidate score needs new A_b. Good. Precompute current transforms once per iteration.

Functions top-level:
```
def _entropy_precompute(a, lam, L):
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
   cmax=c.max()
   scale=max(lam*cmax, _TINY)
   v=1/(c+scale); cap=1/scale; v=np.minimum(v, cap)
   V=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
   h=irfft(conj(A)*V,L)[:n]
   h=np.maximum(h,_TINY)
   return A,c,cmax,scale,v,h
```
Then `_entropy_candidate_from_pre(pre, step)` uses h to new b, then score b with FFT. But to compute score b, we can use rfft b and eq. This reduces to per iteration: rfft a + irfft c + rfft v + irfft h + (for attempts rfft b + irfft c). If two attempts: 2 extra candidate transforms. Good. However precompute returns large arrays A, c, v, h; memory okay. We need n,M.

`entropy_refine` loop:
```
def entropy_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
    n=len(a); L=fft_len(n); M=2*n-1
    deadline = ...
    best=a; best_score=score_fast(a)
    lam=0.1; step=0.1; it=0
    stuck=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
        A, c, cmax, scale, v, h = _entropy_precompute(best, lam, L)
        # if cmax <= tiny break
        # attempts
        improved=False
        for sfrac in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
            s=step*sfrac
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(best,_TINY)) + s*(np.log(h)-np.mean(np.log(h)))
            b=softmax(loga)
            sc=score_fast(b)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                best=b; best_score=sc; stuck=0
                if sfrac < 1.0: step = max(0.02, step*1.0) # maybe keep
                else: step=min(1.0, step*1.05)
                improved=True
                break
        if not improved:
            step=max(0.02, step*0.75)
            stuck +=1
            if stuck % 50 == 0:
                # perturb lam/step
                lam = 0.2 if lam < 0.05 else 0.01
                step = 0.1
                if stuck > 300:
                    # small random kick?
                    noise = 0.005*np.random...; b=(best*(1+noise)); normalize; sc; if sc<best_score accept
        # decay lam slowly
        if it % 20 == 19:
            lam = max(0.002, lam*0.97)
            if lam <=0.003:
                # maybe increase step? no
        it +=1
        if it % 100 == 0: print
    return best,best_score
```
Using `score_fast` for candidate uses another conv; okay. But in loop, we call `score_fast(b)` twice if attempts fail; each 2 transforms. Plus precompute 4 transforms. For 1000 iterations ~6000 transforms, okay. If 10000, 60000 transforms maybe 30s? 1 CPU maybe okay within 2400. Fine.

But `score_fast` recomputes FFT length and maybe L; we can pass L to avoid. Define `score_fast(a, L=None)`; if L None compute. Then in loop use `score_fast(b, L)`. Good.

`_entropy_precompute` should cap h? It is positive; due rounding h values nonnegative. Use `h=np.maximum(h, _TINY)`.

def subgradient_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
  n,L; deadline; best, best_score
  step=0.05; it=0; stuck=0
  while time < deadline-1:
     c=score_fast? Need c and cmax. Compute conv with rfft/irfft directly.
     cmax=c.max()
     # get weights: alternate between LSE and topk
     if it%2==0:
        tau=max(1e-12, 0.05*cmax)
        ex=np.exp((c-cmax)/tau); w=ex/ex.sum()
     else:
        w=top_weights(c, min(50,n))
     A=rfft(a,L); W=rfft(w,L); h=irfft(conj(A)*W)[:n]; g=2h
     # attempts additive + multiplicative
     improved=False
     mean_a=a.mean()
     for sfrac in (1,0.5,0.25):
        delta=step*sfrac*mean_a/(mean(abs(g))+eps)
        b=np.maximum(a-delta*g,0); s=b.sum(); if s>0 b/=s
        sc=score_fast(b,L); if sc<best_score: accept; improved=True; break
     if not improved:
        # multiplicative
        centered=g - g.mean(); std=centered.std()
        if std>0:
           for sfrac in (1,0.5):
               b=softmax(log(a+eps) - step*sfrac*centered/(std+eps)); sc=score_fast; if improve accept
        # reduce step
     if not improved: step*0.8; stuck++ ... sometimes reset
     # decay step slowly
     if it%100==99: step=max(0.005, step*0.99)
```

Helper functions:
```
def _clip_normalize(v):
    a=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a=np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64)
        s=1.0
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a
```
If len 0: return empty? propose handles.

`_fft_length(n)`:
```
def _fft_length(n):
    m = 2*n - 1
    return 1 << (max(1, m).bit_length())
```
If m is 1 (n=1) bit_length 1 ->2; okay.

```
def _conv_fft(a, L=None):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if L is None: L=_fft_length(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny negative
    if c.size:
       # c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)? Conv of nonnegative should nonnegative; roundoff negative maybe. Clipping may affect score tiny. Use np.where(c<0 & c>-1e-12,0)
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Is clipping negative to 0 okay? If due roundoff only tiny. For future weights, nonnegative. Good.

```
def _score_fast(a, L=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return float('inf')
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300: return inf
   if L is None: L=_fft_length(n)
   c=_conv_fft(a,L)
   if c.size==0: return inf
   m=float(c.max())
   if not np.isfinite(m): return inf
   return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```
Since local a usually normalized, but this general.

`_external_score(a)`:
```
def _external_score(a):
    try:
       lst=[float(x) for x in a]
       val=evaluate_sequence(lst)
       if np.isfinite(val): return float(val)
       return float('inf')
    except Exception:
       return _score_fast(a)
```

`_resample_sequence(a,n)`:
```
def _resample_sequence(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float); m=len(a)
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   if m==n: return _clip_normalize(a)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   b=np.maximum(b,0)
   s=b.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300: b=np.ones(n)/n
   else: b/=s
   return b
```

`_softmax(x)`:
```
def _softmax(x):
   x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
   x=x-x.max()
   ex=np.exp(x)
   s=ex.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
      return np.ones(x.size)/x.size
   return ex/s
```

`_correlate_weights(v,a,L)`:
```
def _correlate_weights(v,a,L):
   n=len(a)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   V=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*V, L)[:n]
   h=np.maximum(h, 0.0)
   return h
```
Could use nonsymmetric due roundoff; okay.

`_make_top_weights(c,k, power=1.0)`:
```
def _make_top_weights(c,k):
   M=len(c); k=min(k,M)
   if k <=0: return np.ones(M)/M
   # use argpartition for top k
   if k == M: return np.full(M,1.0/M)
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
   vals=c[idx]
   denom=vals.sum()
   if denom <= _TINY:
       w=np.zeros(M); w[idx]=1.0/k; return w
   w=np.zeros(M); w[idx]=vals/denom; return w
```
If values near equal, okay. For top k including all if k=M.

Global opt variables:
```
_OPT_N=1; _OPT_L=2; _OPT_M=1; _OPT_TAU=1e-8
_OPT_X=None; _OPT_A=None; _OPT_FA=None; _OPT_C=None
_OPT_VAL=float('inf')
```
Declare global in functions.

`_opt_setup(n,L,M,tau)`: could just set in lbfgs.

`_opt_objective(x)`:
```
def _opt_objective(x):
   global ...
   n=_OPT_N; L=_OPT_L; M=_OPT_M; tau=_OPT_TAU
   a=_softmax(x)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
   c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
   _OPT_X=x.copy(); _OPT_A=a; _OPT_FA=A; _OPT_C=c
   cmax=float(c.max())
   if tau <= _TINY:
       return cmax
   t=(c-cmax)/tau
   ex=np.exp(t)
   s=ex.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       # fallback exact
       return cmax
   val=cmax + tau*math.log(s)
   _OPT_VAL=val
   return val
```
Need `math.log`.

`_opt_gradient(x)`:
```
def _opt_gradient(x):
   global ...
   n=_OPT_N; L=_OPT_L; M=_OPT_M; tau=_OPT_TAU
   use_cache=False
   if _OPT_X is not None and _OPT_A is not None and _OPT_FA is not None and _OPT_C is not None:
       if _OPT_X.shape == x.shape:
           # use allclose but may be expensive; okay
           if np.allclose(_OPT_X, x, rtol=0.0, atol=1e-15):
               a=_OPT_A; A=_OPT_FA; c=_OPT_C; use_cache=True
   if not use_cache:
       a=_softmax(x)
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   if tau <= _TINY:
       # subgradient of exact max: uniform weights over max lags
       idx=np.flatnonzero(np.isclose(c,cmax,atol=max(1e-15, 1e-12*cmax), rtol=1e-12))
       w=np.zeros(M); 
       if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))]); w[idx]=1.0/idx.size
       else: w[idx]=1.0/idx.size
   else:
       t=(c-cmax)/tau
       ex=np.exp(t); s=ex.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
           idx=np.argmax(c); w=np.zeros(M); w[idx]=1.0
       else:
           w=ex/s
   V=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*V, L)[:n]
   h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
   g_a=2.0*h
   dot=float(np.dot(a,g_a))
   g_x=a*(g_a-dot)
   # remove nonfinite
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(g_x)):
       g_x=np.nan_to_num(g_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return g_x
```
If tau very small, LSE weights exactly max via exp underflow; we could use LSE with min tau 1e-10; symmetric ties okay. The `tau <=_TINY` branch not used.

`_run_minimize_once(x, tau, maxiter)`:
Set global tau, call minimize. Need avoid closure. We can call minimize directly in lbfgs.

`_lbfgs_refine(a, time_limit, tag)`:
```
def _lbfgs_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
    global _OPT_N, ...
    if time_limit <= 5: return a, _score_fast(a)
    n=len(a); L=_fft_length(n); M=2*n-1
    global OPT; set
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(a,L)
    cmax=best_score/(2*n) if n>0 else 1
    if cmax <= _TINY: return best_a,best_score
    tau_list = [cmax*f for f in (0.25,0.1,0.04,0.015,0.006,0.002,0.0008)]
    # filter > min_tau
    min_tau=1e-10
    x=np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    start_tau=time.monotonic()
    for idx,t in enumerate(tau_list):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       if t < min_tau: t=min_tau
       _OPT_TAU=t
       # clear cache
       _OPT_X=None; _OPT_A=None; _OPT_FA=None; _OPT_C=None
       # maxiter depends on remaining; maybe 250 + (n small)
       maxiter = 300
       # if n>8000, 150
       if n > 8000: maxiter=150
       try:
          res = minimize(_opt_objective, x, jac=_opt_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B',
                         options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 0.0, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'maxcor': 30, 'maxls': 50})
          if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
             x = res.x
       except Exception as e:
          print('lbfgs error', e)
       a_new = _softmax(x)
       sc_new = _score_fast(a_new, L)
       if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
          best_a=a_new; best_score=sc_new
       # Maybe external check
       if idx % 2 == 1:
          ext=_external_score(a_new)
          if np.isfinite(ext) and ext < best_score:
             best_score=ext
       print(...)
    # final external? maybe caller.
    return best_a, best_score
```
Potential issue: `minimize` may change x to values causing softmax underflow but okay. If res.x length differs? no.

Time within minimize uncontrolled; maxiter 300. For n=4806, likely okay. For n=4096, faster. For alt maybe n=4096. If n=1? no.

`_entropy_precompute(a,lam,L)` as above. Need n,M.
```
def _entropy_precompute(a, lam, L):
   n=len(a); M=2*n-1
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   scale=max(lam*cmax, 1e-300)
   den=c+scale
   # if den <=0? no
   v=1.0/den
   cap=1.0/scale
   v=np.minimum(v, cap)
   V=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*V,L)[:n]
   h=np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
   return A,c,cmax,scale,v,h
```
We don't actually use c/scale/v later except maybe debug; h used. We return A? not used outside. Maybe don't return A/c/v to save memory? But function call in loop just h. But we need cmax for print/score? We can compute current score maybe. However `entropy_refine` has best_score; cmax from current can be used if score modified? We'll return cmax. Could return h, cmax only.
```
def _entropy_h(a,lam,L): ... return h,cmax
```
Simpler and less memory. But inside candidate we will compute score with b. We don't need c. Let's do:
```
def _entropy_direction(a, lam, L):
    A=rfft; c=irfft; cmax; scale; v; V; h; return h,cmax
```
Good.

`_entropy_refine(a, time_limit, tag, L=None)`:
```
def _entropy_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
   if time_limit <=5: return a,_score_fast(a)
   n=len(a); L=_fft_length(n); deadline=...
   best=a.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(a,L)
   lam=0.1; step=0.1; it=0; stuck=0
   rng = np.random.default_rng(2024)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
       h,cmax = _entropy_direction(best, lam, L)
       if cmax <= _TINY: break
       logh = np.log(h) - np.mean(np.log(h))
       improved=False
       # attempts: current step, half, quarter
       for sfrac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
           s=step*sfrac
           if s <= 0: break
           loga=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)) + s*logh
           b=_softmax(loga)
           sc=_score_fast(b,L)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
               best=b; best_score=sc; improved=True
               if sfrac >= 1.0: step=min(1.5, step*1.08)
               else: step=max(step, s*1.2)
               break
       if not improved:
           step=max(0.02, step*0.75)
           stuck +=1
           if stuck == 30:
               # random small kick to escape
               noise = 0.02*rng.normal(size=n)
               b=best*(1.0+noise); b=np.maximum(b,0); b/_score? normalize
               sc=_score_fast(b,L)
               if sc < best_score:
                   best=b; best_score=sc; stuck=0
           if stuck > 200:
               # reset parameters
               if lam <0.2: lam=0.2
               else: lam=0.005
               step=0.1
               stuck=0
       # decay lam toward 0.002
       if it % 20 == 19:
           lam = max(0.002, lam*0.985)
       it +=1
       if it % 200 == 0: print progress
   # final external update
   return best,best_score
```
`step` max 1.5; can cause big changes but line search. Maybe cap 1.0.

Does `_entropy_direction` use current best `a` normalized? yes. If best from `_softmax` sum1. Good.

`_subgradient_refine(a, time_limit, tag)`:
We'll implement with L. Use top weights and LSE weights. Could share `_correlate_weights`, but we need x? no.
```
def _subgradient_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
   deadline...
   best=a; best_score=score_fast
   n,L; step=0.05; it=0; stuck=0
   while now < deadline-1:
      A=rfft(best,L); c=irfft(A*A,L)[:M]; cmax=c.max()
      if cmax<=0: break
      if it % 2 == 0:
         tau=max(1e-12, 0.03*cmax)
         ex=np.exp((c-cmax)/tau); s=ex.sum(); w=ex/s
      else:
         w=_make_top_weights(c, min(64, n))
      W=rfft(w,L); h=irfft(conj(A)*W,L)[:n]; g=2h
      # if g max zero break
      improved=False
      mean_a=best.mean()
      mean_g=float(np.mean(np.abs(g)))
      if mean_g > 1e-300:
         for sfrac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
             delta=step*sfrac*mean_a/mean_g
             b=best - delta*g
             b=np.maximum(b,0.0); s=b.sum()
             if s <= 0: continue
             b/=s
             sc=score_fast(b,L)
             if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                 best=b; best_score=sc; improved=True
                 if sfrac >=1: step=min(0.5, step*1.1)
                 else: step=max(step, sfrac)*1.1
                 break
      if not improved:
         # multiplicative
         centered=g - g.mean()
         std=float(np.std(centered))
         if std > 1e-300:
            normc = centered/std
            for sfrac in (1.0,0.5):
                s=step*0.5*sfrac
                loga=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)) - s*normc
                b=_softmax(loga)
                sc=score_fast(b,L)
                if sc < best_score -1e-15:
                    best=b; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
         if not improved:
             step=max(0.005, step*0.8)
             stuck +=1
             if stuck == 40:
                # random additive small?
                rng noise? but default_rng defined? use local static? top-level RNG? We can create in function.
             if stuck > 250:
                step=0.05; stuck=0
      else:
         stuck=0
      it +=1
      if it % 100 ==0: print
   return best,best_score
```

    start=time.monotonic()

    # get height
    ...

    half = _half_binomial(main_len)

       mix1 = 0.7*h_full + 0.3*half; cands append if finite
       mix2 = 0.5*h_full + 0.5*half[::-1]; append
       cands.append(make_heuristic(main_len))
    else:
       cands.extend variations of half
    # pick best quick score
    best_a= cands[0]; best_sc = score_fast
    for cand in cands[1:]: sc=score_fast; if sc<best_sc: update
    print initial
    # main refine with remaining *0.62 or fixed 1500
    remain=deadline-time
    t_main=min(remain*0.62, 1600.0)
    best_a,best_sc=_refine_sequence(best_a, t_main, 'main')
    # update external

    # alt length 4096

       a_alt=_resample_sequence(best_a, 4096)

       a_alt,sc_alt=_refine_sequence(a_alt,t_alt,'alt4096')
       if sc_alt<best_sc: best_a=a_alt; best_sc=sc_alt

    # random restart around best maybe 150s

    # final polish on global best

**Heuristic functions**:
```
def _half_binomial(n):
    if n <=0: return np.array([])
    a=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        a[i] = a[i-1] * ((2.0*i - 1.0)/(2.0*i))
    s=a.sum()
    if s>0: a/=s
    return a
```
For i integer, (2.0*i). Good.

`_u_shape_heuristic(n)`:
```
def _u_shape_heuristic(n):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    edge=np.minimum(i, n-1.0-i)
    a=1.0/np.sqrt(edge+1.0)
    # add slight asymmetry
    a = a * (1.0 + 0.1*np.sin(2*np.pi*i/max(1,n)))
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    return _clip_normalize(a)
```

def _initial_candidates(main_len, height_a):
   cands=[]
   if height_a is not None:
      cands.append(height_a)
   half = _half_binomial(main_len)
   cands.append(half)
   if height_a is not None:
      mix = 0.8*height_a + 0.2*half
      cands.append(_clip_normalize(mix))
      mix2 = 0.5*height_a + 0.5*half[::-1]
      cands.append(_clip_normalize(mix2))
   # symmetric U
   i=np.arange(main_len)
   edge=np.minimum(i, main_len-1-i)
   u=1/np.sqrt(edge+1)
   cands.append(_clip_normalize(u))
   # arcsine both edges
   x=np.arange(main_len,dtype=float)
   arb=1/np.sqrt((x+1)*(main_len-x))
   cands.append(_clip_normalize(arb))
   # slight non-sym half: 0.9 half + 0.1 reversed
   cands.append(_clip_normalize(0.9*half + 0.1*half[::-1]))
   # score and pick
```

**Let's craft final code now mentally, ensuring no syntax errors.

Code skeleton:
```
"""
Optimize nonnegative coefficient sequence to minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.
Uses analytical shape initialization (truncated binomial square root), entropy/iterative-scaling
updates to flatten convolution coefficients, L-BFGS with log-sum-exp smoothed max, and
active-set subgradient polishing.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_TINY = 1e-300
_EPS = 1e-15
# globals
_OPT_N=1
_OPT_L=2
_OPT_M=1

Implement `_clip_normalize`:
```
def _clip_normalize(v):
    if v is None:
        return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= _TINY:
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.array([1.0])
        a=np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s=1.0
    a = a / s
    return a
```
If original v is list of bool? converts.

`_fft_length`:
```
def _fft_length(n):
    m=2*int(n)-1
    if m <=1: return 2
    return 1 << (m.bit_length())
```
If m power of two, bit_length gives exponent+1, e.g., m=8 -> bit_length 4 ->16 (too large). We need smallest power of two >= m: `1 << (m-1).bit_length()` if m>1. Example m=9611: (9610).bit_length=14 ->16384. If m=8: (7).bit_length=3 ->8. Good. Use:
```
if m <=1: return 1
return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
For n=1, m=1 -> 1; rfft length 1? Conv length1. But n≥2. Use if m<2 return 2. Fine.

`_conv_fft`:
```
def _conv_fft(a, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if L is None: L=_fft_length(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
       # only clip negative tiny
       c = np.where(c < 0.0, np.where(c > -1e-12, 0.0, c), c) # hmm simple maximum
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Using maximum all negative to 0; if due serious negative (shouldn't), clips. Good.

`_score_fast`:
```
def _score_fast(a, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= _TINY: return float('inf')
    if L is None: L=_fft_length(n)
    c=_conv_fft(a,L)
    if c.size ==0: return float('inf')
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```

`_external_score`:
```
def _external_score(a):
    try:
        lst=[float(x) for x in np.asarray(a).ravel()]
        val=evaluate_sequence(lst)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return float(val)
        return float('inf')
    except Exception:
        return _score_fast(a)
```

`_resample_sequence` as above.

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size==0: return np.array([])
    x=x - np.max(x)
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=float(ex.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _TINY:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64)/x.size
    return ex/s
```

`_correlate_weights`:
```
def _correlate_weights(v,a,L):
    n=a.size
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    V=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(F)*V,L)[:n]
    h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
    return h
```

`_make_top_weights`:
```
def _make_top_weights(c,k):
    M=c.size
    if M==0: return np.array([])
    k=int(min(max(k,1),M))
    if k >= M:
       return np.full(M,1.0/M)
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    vals=c[idx]
    s=float(vals.sum())
    w=np.zeros(M)
    if s > _TINY:
       w[idx]=vals/s
    else:
       w[idx]=1.0/k
    return w
```

`_opt_objective` and gradient:
```
def _opt_objective(x):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_L, _OPT_M, _OPT_TAU, _OPT_X, _OPT_A, _OPT_FA, _OPT_C
    n=_OPT_N; L=_OPT_L; M=_OPT_M; tau=_OPT_TAU
    a=_softmax(x)
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:M]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    _OPT_X=x.copy()
    _OPT_A=a
    _OPT_FA=F
    _OPT_C=c
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= _TINY:
       return cmax
    t=(c-cmax)/tau
    ex=np.exp(t)
    s=float(ex.sum())
    if s <= _TINY or not np.isfinite(s):
       return cmax
    return float(cmax + tau*math.log(s))
```

Gradient as above. In cache check, `np.allclose` on large might be 4806; okay. Use `if _OPT_X is not None and ... and (x is _OPT_X or np.allclose(...))`? `x is _OPT_X` maybe if same object? Stored copy, so false. Use allclose. If x has different shape, skip.
```
def _opt_gradient(x):
   global ...
   n=...; 
   use=False
   if _OPT_X is not None and _OPT_A is not None and _OPT_FA is not None and _OPT_C is not None:
      if _OPT_X.shape == x.shape:
          # if exactly equal? use np.array_equal for speed? SciPy may pass same values; array_equal O(n) cheaper than allclose. Use array_equal.
          if np.array_equal(_OPT_X, x):
              use=True
   if not use:
      x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
      a=_softmax(x)
      F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
      c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:M]
      c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   else:
      a=_OPT_A; F=_OPT_FA; c=_OPT_C
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if tau <= _TINY: ...
   else:
      t=(c-cmax)/tau; ex=np.exp(t); s=sum; if bad idx max; else w=ex/s
   V=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(F)*V,L)[:n]
   h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
   g_a=2.0*h
   dot=float(np.dot(a,g_a))
   g_x=a*(g_a-dot)
   g_x=np.nan_to_num(g_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return g_x
```
`np.array_equal` may fail if x copy but values exactly same floats; L-BFGS passes same array, should. If it passes a slightly different due to conversion, recompute.

`_lbfgs_refine` uses globals set.
```
def _lbfgs_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
   global _OPT_N, ...
   a=_clip_normalize(a)
   n=a.size
   if n<2 or time_limit<5: return a, _score_fast(a)
   L=_fft_length(n); M=2*n-1
   _OPT_N=n; _OPT_L=L; _OPT_M=M
   best=a; best_score=_score_fast(a,L)
   cmax=best_score/(2.0*n) if 2*n>0 else 1.0
   if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= _TINY: return best,best_score
   x0=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   deadlines = [(0.25,250), (0.1,250), (0.04,300), (0.015,300), (0.006,300), (0.002,300), (0.0008,300)]
   # min tau
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_limit
   min_tau=1e-10
   for frac,maxiter in tau_fractions:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       t = max(min_tau, cmax*frac)
       _OPT_TAU=t
       _OPT_X=None; _OPT_A=None; _OPT_FA=None; _OPT_C=None
       # If remaining time small, maxiter reduce
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 20: maxiter=50
       elif rem < 120: maxiter=120
       try:
          res=minimize(_opt_objective, x0, jac=_opt_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B',
                       options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':0.0,'gtol':1e-12,'maxcor':25,'maxls':40})
          if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
              x0=res.x
       except Exception as e:
          print('lbfgs exception', e)
       a_new=_softmax(x0)
       sc_new=_score_fast(a_new,L)
       if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
          best=a_new; best_score=sc_new
       # maybe if cmax changed, base tau for next fractions should update? Use initial cmax or update? If score improved, cmax_new smaller. Use current best_score for tau to keep relevant.
       if np.isfinite(best_score):
           cmax = max(min_tau, best_score/(2.0*n))
       print(...)
   return best,best_score
```

`_entropy_refine`:
Need `L` for score. Use `_score_fast(b,L)`.
```
def _entropy_refine(a,time_limit,tag):
   a=_clip_normalize(a)
   n=a.size
   if n<2 or time_limit<5: return a,_score_fast(a)
   L=_fft_length(n)
   best=a; best_score=_score_fast(a,L)
   deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
   lam=0.1; step=0.15; it=0; stuck=0
   rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
   logh_cache=None
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
       A=np.fft.rfft(best,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax <= _TINY: break
       scale=max(lam*cmax, 1e-300)
       v=1.0/(c + scale)
       cap=1.0/scale
       v=np.minimum(v, cap)
       V=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
       h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*V,L)[:n]
       h=np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
       logh=np.log(h); logh=logh - logh.mean()
       improved=False
       for sfrac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
           s=step*sfrac
           if s <= 1e-8: break
           loga=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)) + s*logh
           b=_softmax(loga)
           sc=_score_fast(b,L)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
               best=b; best_score=sc; improved=True
               if sfrac >= 1.0: step=min(1.0, step*1.06)
               else: step=max(step, s*1.1)
               stuck=0
               break
       if not improved:
           step=max(0.02, step*0.8)
           stuck +=1
           if stuck == 30:
               noise=0.02*rng.normal(size=n)
               b=best*(1.0+noise)
               b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
               sb=b.sum()
               if sb > _TINY:
                   b=b/sb
                   sc=_score_fast(b,L)
                   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                       best=b; best_score=sc; stuck=0
           if stuck > 250:
               if lam > 0.05:
                   lam=0.05
               else:
                   lam=0.2
               step=0.15
               stuck=0
       it +=1
       if it % 25 == 0:
           lam=max(0.002, lam*0.98)
       if it % 250 == 0:
           print(f"[{tag}] entropy it={it} score={best_score:.8f} lam={lam:.5f} step={step:.4f}")
   return best,best_score
```
This recomputes A/c each iteration. Good.

Could we use current best_score external? no.

`_subgradient_refine`:
```
def _subgradient_refine(a,time_limit,tag):
   a=_clip_normalize(a)
   n=a.size
   if n<2 or time_limit<5: return a,_score_fast(a)
   L=_fft_length(n); M=2*n-1
   best=a; best_score=_score_fast(a,L)
   deadline=...
   step=0.05; it=0; stuck=0
   rng=np.random.default_rng(999)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
       F=np.fft.rfft(best,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:M]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax <= _TINY: break
       if it % 2 == 0:
          tau=max(1e-12, 0.02*cmax)
          t=(c-cmax)/tau
          ex=np.exp(t); s=ex.sum()
          if s<=_TINY: w=_make_top_weights(c,1)
          else: w=ex/s
       else:
          w=_make_top_weights(c, min(64, n))
       V=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
       h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(F)*V,L)[:n]
       g=2.0*np.maximum(h,0.0)
       improved=False
       mean_a=best.mean()
       mean_g=float(np.mean(np.abs(g)))
       if mean_g > _TINY:
          for sfrac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
              delta=step*sfrac*mean_a/mean_g
              if delta <= 0: continue
              b=best - delta*g
              b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
              sb=float(b.sum())
              if sb <= _TINY: continue
              b=b/sb
              sc=_score_fast(b,L)
              if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                  best=b; best_score=sc; improved=True
                  if sfrac >=1: step=min(0.5, step*1.1)
                  else: step=max(step, sfrac*step? no, s=step*sfrac; step=s*1.1)
                  break
       if not improved:
           centered=g - g.mean()
           std=float(np.std(centered))
           if std > _TINY:
              normc=centered/std
              for sfrac in (1.0,0.5):
                  s=step*0.5*sfrac
                  loga=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)) - s*normc
                  b=_softmax(loga)
                  sc=_score_fast(b,L)
                  if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score -1e-14:
                      best=b; best_score=sc; improved=True
                      break
           if not improved:
               step=max(0.005, step*0.8)
               stuck +=1
               if stuck == 40:
                   noise=0.01*rng.normal(size=n)
                   b=best*(1.0+noise); b=np.maximum(b,0); sb=b.sum()
                   if sb>0: b/=sb; sc=score; if sc<best update
               if stuck > 300:
                   step=0.05; stuck=0
       else:
           stuck=0
       it +=1
       if it % 25 ==0:
           step=max(0.005, step*0.995) # slow decay? But step may increase on improve. add slight decay
       if it % 250 ==0: print
   return best,best_score
```
Note in improved branch for sfrac<1: `step = max(step, s*1.1)` where s=step_old*sfrac. If step_old=0.05, sfrac=0.5 -> s=0.025 -> step becomes max(0.05,0.0275)=0.05, unchanged. Should maybe set step = s / sfrac? old. If a smaller step worked, current step may be too large; we can leave unchanged or reduce slightly. Use `step = max(0.005, step*0.9)`. Simpler: after any accept, `step = min(0.5, step*1.05)`. If a half step accepted, maybe keep or increase. Fine.

Additive step update: if sfrac<1 accept, maybe current step too high? But smaller step worked; keep step? It might again try full and fail. We can set `step = max(0.005, step*0.8)` on small-step accept. Not critical.

**Potential issue: `_subgradient_refine` `delta=step*sfrac*mean_a/mean_g`; if `mean_g` much smaller than typical because g zero in many entries, delta larger. okay.

def _refine_sequence(a, time_limit, tag):
    a=_clip_normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2 or time_limit<=10:
        sc=_score_fast(a)
        return a, sc
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+time_limit
    best=a; best_score=_score_fast(a)
    # helper no closures: sequential
    allowed = min(time_limit*0.18, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2)
    if allowed > 10:
        best, sc = _entropy_refine(best, allowed, tag+' e1')
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: best_score=sc
    allowed = min(time_limit*0.34, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2)
    if allowed > 15:
        best, sc = _lbfgs_refine(best, allowed, tag+' lb1')
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: best_score=sc
    allowed = min(time_limit*0.16, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: best_score=sc
    allowed = min(time_limit*0.16, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2)
    if allowed > 15:
        best, sc = _lbfgs_refine(best, allowed, tag+' lb2')
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: best_score=sc
    allowed = min(time_limit*0.10, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2)
    if allowed > 10:

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: best_score=sc

       if allowed > 5:
           best,sc=_subgradient_refine(best, allowed, tag+' s2')
           if finite update
    return best,best_score

**` _heuristic_candidates`**:
```
def _quick_pick_initial(main_len, height_a):
    cands=[]
    if height_a is not None and len(height_a)==main_len:
        cands.append(height_a)
    half=_half_binomial(main_len)
    cands.append(half)
    if height_a is not None:
        cands.append(_clip_normalize(0.8*height_a + 0.2*half))
        cands.append(_clip_normalize(0.5*height_a + 0.5*half[::-1]))
        cands.append(_clip_normalize(0.6*half + 0.4*half[::-1]))
    # u edge
    i=np.arange(main_len,dtype=float)
    edge=np.minimum(i, main_len-1.0-i)
    u=1.0/np.sqrt(edge+1.0)
    cands.append(_clip_normalize(u))
    # arcsine both ends
    b=1.0/np.sqrt(np.maximum((i+1.0)*(main_len-i),1e-12))
    cands.append(_clip_normalize(b))
    # non-sym half rev mix
    cands.append(_clip_normalize(0.9*half + 0.1*half[::-1]))
    best=cands[0]; best_sc=_score_fast(best)
    for c in cands[1:]:
       sc=_score_fast(c)
       if sc < best_sc: best,sc=c
    return best,best_sc,half
```
Need avoid duplicate huge? okay.

But if main_len=4806, `half[::-1]` is spike at end; mix 0.9 half + 0.1 rev. Good.

```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 120.0)
    hraw = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    height_a = None
    if hraw is not None:
       try:
          harr=np.asarray(hraw,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          if harr.size >0:
             height_a=_clip_normalize(harr)
       except Exception:
          height_a=None
    if height_a is not None:
       n=len(height_a)
       if n < 1024:
           main_len=4806
           a_height=_resample_sequence(height_a, main_len)
       elif n > 8192:
           main_len=8192
           a_height=_resample_sequence(height_a, main_len)
       else:
           main_len=n
           a_height=height_a
    else:
       main_len=4806
       a_height=None
    if a_height is None:
       a_height=_half_binomial(main_len)
    initial, init_score, half = _initial_candidates(main_len, a_height)
    best_a=initial; best_score=init_score
    # external initial
    ext=_external_score(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(ext): best_score=ext
    print('initial...')
    remain=deadline - time.monotonic()
    t_main = min(remain * 0.62, 1600.0)
    if t_main > 30:
       best_a, sc = _refine_sequence(best_a, t_main, 'main')
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: best_score=sc
       ext=_external_score(best_a)
       if np.isfinite(ext):
          if ext < best_score: best_score=ext
          # If external lower than sc? update? best_a same
    # alt 4096
    remain=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remain > 600:
       a_alt = _resample_sequence(best_a, 4096)
       t_alt = min(360.0, remain*0.20)
       a_alt, sc_alt = _refine_sequence(a_alt, t_alt, 'alt4096')
       ext_alt = _external_score(a_alt)
       # compare external if finite else sc_alt
       score_alt = ext_alt if np.isfinite(ext_alt) else sc_alt
       if score_alt < best_score:
           best_a, best_score = a_alt, score_alt
    # alt half at main_len if current length == main_len
    remain=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remain > 700 and abs(len(best_a)-main_len) < 100: # or always
       a_half = _half_binomial(main_len)
       # small perturbation
       rng=np.random.default_rng(555)
       a_half = a_half * (1.0 + 0.01*rng.normal(size=main_len))
       a_half=_clip_normalize(a_half)
       t_half=min(300.0, remain*0.15)
       a_half, sc_half=_refine_sequence(a_half, t_half, 'half')
       ext_half=_external_score(a_half)
       score_half=ext_half if np.isfinite(ext_half) else sc_half
       if score_half < best_score:
           best_a, best_score = a_half, score_half
    # random kick around best
    remain=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remain > 500:
       rng=np.random.default_rng(777)
       n=len(best_a)
       a_kick=best_a*(1.0 + 0.02*rng.normal(size=n))
       a_kick=_clip_normalize(a_kick)
       t_kick=min(200.0, remain*0.15)
       a_kick, sc_kick=_refine_sequence(a_kick, t_kick, 'kick')
       ext_kick=_external_score(a_kick)
       score_kick=ext_kick if np.isfinite(ext_kick) else sc_kick
       if score_kick < best_score:
           best_a,best_score=a_kick, score_kick
    # final polish with external-aware? Use subgrad length whatever
    remain=deadline-time.monotonic()-10
    if remain > 20:
       t_fin=min(120.0, remain)
       best_a, sc_fin = _subgradient_refine(best_a, t_fin, 'final')
       ext_fin=_external_score(best_a)
       if np.isfinite(ext_fin) and ext_fin < best_score:
          best_score=ext_fin
    # final external
    final_ext = _external_score(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(final_ext):
        # if external differs, accept best_a (same). But if final_ext > best_score? keep best_score local? For printing, use final_ext maybe.
        pass
    out=[float(v) for v in np.asarray(best_a).ravel()]
    # ensure valid
    if not out or not all(np.isfinite(v) and v>=0 for v in out):
        out=[float(v) for v in _half_binomial(4806)]
    final_eval=_external_score(out)
    print(f"final length={len(out)} local={_score_fast(out)} external={final_eval}")
    return out
```

But if main_len large, fine.

**Potential issue: `_initial_candidates` uses local score to pick initial; if external and local differ by tiny, okay.

**Potential issue: If height sequence exists but length not main_len due resample, mix with half length: We set a_height resampled to main_len. Good.

```python
"""
Optimize nonnegative coefficient sequences to minimize
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Algorithm:
- Start from the provided state-of-the-art sequence when available, and
  from analytic low-convolution-shape candidates (truncated binomial square root,
  edge-singular shapes).
- Use an entropy / iterative-scaling update to flatten the convolution
  coefficients: maximize sum_k log(c_k), c = a*a.
- Use smoothed log-sum-exp L-BFGS on the exact convolution maximum.
- Finish with active-set subgradient projection and multiplicative polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small global state for the L-BFGS objective/gradient without closures.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
_TINY = 1e-300
_EPS = 1e-15

_OPT_N = 1
_OPT_L = 2
_OPT_M = 1
_OPT_TAU = 1e-8
_OPT_DEADLINE = 0.0

_OPT_X = None
_OPT_A = None
_OPT_FA = None
_OPT_C = None


class _OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass


def _opt_callback(xk):
    global _OPT_DEADLINE
    if time.monotonic() > _OPT_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _clip_normalize(v):
    if v is None:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s <= _TINY:
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0

    return a / s


def _fft_length(n):
    n = int(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if m <= 1:
        return 2
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _conv_fft(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if L is None:
        L = _fft_length(n)

    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score_fast(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= _TINY:
        return float("inf")

    if L is None:
        L = _fft_length(n)

    c = _conv_fft(a, L)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")

    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _external_score(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return float(val)
        return float("inf")
    except Exception:
        return _score_fast(a)


def _authoritative_score(a, local_score):
    ext = _external_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(ext):
        return float(ext)
    if np.isfinite(local_score):
        return float(local_score)
    return float("inf")


def _resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    m = a.size

    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    if m == n:
        return _clip_normalize(a)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= _TINY:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return b / s


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(ex.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _TINY:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / x.size

    return ex / s


def _correlate_weights(v, a, L):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size

    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    V = np.fft.rfft(v, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(F) * V, L)[:n]
    return np.maximum(h, 0.0)


def _make_top_weights(c, k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    M = c.size
    if M == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    k = int(min(max(k, 1), M))
    if k >= M:
        return np.full(M, 1.0 / M, dtype=np.float64)

    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    vals = c[idx]
    s = float(vals.sum())

    w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    if s > _TINY:
        w[idx] = vals / s
    else:
        w[idx] = 1.0 / k
    return w


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# LSE-smoothed maximum convolution objective for L-BFGS.
# Optimizes over x with a = softmax(x), hence scale is fixed.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _opt_objective(x):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_L, _OPT_M, _OPT_TAU, _OPT_X, _OPT_A, _OPT_FA, _OPT_C

    n = _OPT_N
    L = _OPT_L
    M = _OPT_M
    tau = _OPT_TAU

    a = _softmax(x)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:M]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    _OPT_X = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    _OPT_A = a
    _OPT_FA = F
    _OPT_C = c

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= _TINY:
        return cmax

    ex = np.exp((c - cmax) / tau)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _TINY:
        return cmax

    return float(cmax + tau * math.log(s))


def _opt_gradient(x):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_L, _OPT_M, _OPT_TAU, _OPT_X, _OPT_A, _OPT_FA, _OPT_C

    n = _OPT_N
    L = _OPT_L
    M = _OPT_M
    tau = _OPT_TAU

    use_cache = False
    if _OPT_X is not None and _OPT_A is not None and _OPT_FA is not None and _OPT_C is not None:
        if _OPT_X.shape == x.shape and np.array_equal(_OPT_X, x):
            use_cache = True

    if use_cache:
        a = _OPT_A
        F = _OPT_FA
        c = _OPT_C
    else:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        a = _softmax(x)
        F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:M]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    if tau <= _TINY:
        atol = max(1e-15, 1e-12 * cmax)
        idx = np.flatnonzero(np.isclose(c, cmax, rtol=1e-12, atol=atol))
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
        w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / idx.size
    else:
        ex = np.exp((c - cmax) / tau)
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _TINY:
            w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ex / s

    V = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(F) * V, L)[:n]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)

    grad_a = 2.0 * h
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_x = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad_x = np.nan_to_num(grad_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return grad_x


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# L-BFGS with decreasing LSE temperature.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _lbfgs_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_L, _OPT_M, _OPT_TAU, _OPT_X, _OPT_A, _OPT_FA, _OPT_C, _OPT_DEADLINE

    a = _clip_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit < 8.0:
        return a, _score_fast(a)

    L = _fft_length(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1

    _OPT_N = n
    _OPT_L = L
    _OPT_M = M
    _OPT_TAU = 1e-8

    best_a = a
    best_score = _score_fast(a, L)

    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    cmax = best_score / (2.0 * n)
    min_tau = 1e-10

    x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)

    if n <= 5000:
        base_maxiter = 230
        max_stages = 8
    elif n <= 8192:
        base_maxiter = 140
        max_stages = 6
    else:
        base_maxiter = 90
        max_stages = 4

    tau_fracs = [0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008, 0.004, 0.002]
    tau_fracs = tau_fracs[:max_stages]

    _OPT_DEADLINE = max(start + 2.0, deadline - 2.0)

    for stage, frac in enumerate(tau_fracs):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 10.0:
            break

        tau = max(min_tau, cmax * frac)
        _OPT_TAU = float(tau)
        _OPT_X = None
        _OPT_A = None
        _OPT_FA = None
        _OPT_C = None

        maxiter = base_maxiter
        remaining = deadline - now
        if remaining < 40.0:
            maxiter = min(maxiter, 50)
        elif remaining < 150.0:
            maxiter = min(maxiter, 120)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _opt_objective,
                x0,
                jac=_opt_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                callback=_opt_callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "maxfun": int(max(100, maxiter * 10)),
                    "ftol": 0.0,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxcor": 25,
                    "maxls": 40,
                },
            )
            x_trial = res.x
        except _OptTimeout:
            print(f"[{tag}] lbfgs timeout at stage {stage + 1}, tau={tau:.3e}")
            continue
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{tag}] lbfgs exception: {e}")
            continue

        if x_trial is None or x_trial.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x_trial)):
            continue

        a_trial = _softmax(x_trial)
        sc_trial = _score_fast(a_trial, L)

        if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial < best_score:
            best_a = a_trial
            best_score = sc_trial

        if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial <= best_score * 1.02:
            x0 = x_trial

        if np.isfinite(best_score):
            cmax = max(min_tau, best_score / (2.0 * n))

        print(f"[{tag}] lbfgs stage {stage + 1}/{len(tau_fracs)} tau={tau:.3e} score={best_score:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entropy / iterative scaling update:
# maximize sum_k log c_k, c = a*a, which pushes convolution coefficients flat.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _entropy_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
    a = _clip_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit < 8.0:
        return a, _score_fast(a)

    L = _fft_length(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(a, L)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    lam = 0.10
    step = 0.16
    it = 0
    stuck = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240517)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.5:
        F = np.fft.rfft(best, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:M]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= _TINY:
            break

        scale = max(lam * cmax, 1e-300)
        v = 1.0 / (c + scale)
        cap = 1.0 / scale
        v = np.minimum(v, cap)

        V = np.fft.rfft(v, L)
        h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(F) * V, L)[:n]
        h = np.maximum(h, 1e-300)

        logh_raw = np.log(h)
        logh = logh_raw - float(np.mean(logh_raw))

        improved = False
        for sfrac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            s = step * sfrac
            if s <= 1e-10:
                break

            loga = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300)) + s * logh
            b = _softmax(loga)
            sc = _score_fast(b, L)

            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                best = b
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                if sfrac >= 1.0:
                    step = min(1.0, step * 1.06)
                else:
                    step = max(0.02, step * 0.92)
                stuck = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            step = max(0.02, step * 0.80)
            stuck += 1

            if stuck == 30:
                noise = 0.02 * rng.normal(size=n)
                b = best * (1.0 + noise)
                b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                sb = float(b.sum())
                if sb > _TINY:
                    b = b / sb
                    sc = _score_fast(b, L)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best = b
                        best_score = sc
                        stuck = 0

            if stuck > 250:
                if lam > 0.05:
                    lam = 0.05
                else:
                    lam = 0.20
                step = 0.16
                stuck = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 25 == 0:
            lam = max(0.002, lam * 0.98)

        if it % 250 == 0:
            print(f"[{tag}] entropy it={it} score={best_score:.8f} lam={lam:.5f} step={step:.4f}")

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Active-set subgradient / multiplicative polishing.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _subgradient_refine(a, time_limit, tag):
    a = _clip_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit < 8.0:
        return a, _score_fast(a)

    L = _fft_length(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(a, L)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    step = 0.06
    it = 0
    stuck = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(9753108)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.5:
        F = np.fft.rfft(best, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:M]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= _TINY:
            break

        if it % 2 == 0:
            tau = max(1e-12, 0.02 * cmax)
            ex = np.exp((c - cmax) / tau)
            s = float(ex.sum())
            if s > _TINY:
                w = ex / s
            else:
                w = _make_top_weights(c, 1)
        else:
            w = _make_top_weights(c, min(64, n))

        V = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(F) * V, L)[:n]
        g = 2.0 * np.maximum(h, 0.0)

        improved = False
        mean_a = float(np.mean(best))
        mean_g = float(np.mean(np.abs(g)))

        if mean_g > _TINY and mean_a > _TINY:
            for sfrac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
                delta = step * sfrac * mean_a / mean_g
                if delta <= 0.0:
                    continue

                b = best - delta * g
                b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                sb = float(b.sum())
                if sb <= _TINY:
                    continue

                b = b / sb
                sc = _score_fast(b, L)

                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best = b
                    best_score = sc
                    improved = True
                    if sfrac >= 1.0:
                        step = min(0.5, step * 1.10)
                    else:
                        step = max(0.005, step * 0.90)
                    stuck = 0
                    break

        if not improved:
            centered = g - float(np.mean(g))
            std = float(np.std(centered))

            if std > _TINY:
                normc = centered / std
                for sfrac in (1.0, 0.5):
                    s = 0.5 * step * sfrac
                    if s <= 1e-12:
                        continue

                    loga = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300)) - s * normc
                    b = _softmax(loga)
                    sc = _score_fast(b, L)

                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                        best = b
                        best_score = sc
                        improved = True
                        stay = max(0.005, step * (0.95 if sfrac < 1.0 else 1.05))
                        step = stay
                        break

            if not improved:
                step = max(0.005, step * 0.82)
                stuck += 1

                if stuck == 40:
                    noise = 0.012 * rng.normal(size=n)
                    b = best * (1.0 + noise)
                    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                    sb = float(b.sum())
                    if sb > _TINY:
                        b = b / sb
                        sc = _score_fast(b, L)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                            best = b
                            best_score = sc
                            stuck = 0

                if stuck > 300:
                    step = 0.06
                    stuck = 0
            else:
                stuck = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 30 == 0:
            step = max(0.005, step * 0.995)

        if it % 250 == 0:
            print(f"[{tag}] subgrad it={it} score={best_score:.8f} step={step:.5f}")

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Generic multi-phase-refinement routine.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _refine_sequence(a, time_limit, tag):
    a = _clip_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 15.0:
        return a, _score_fast(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)

    best = a.copy()
    best_local = _score_fast(a)
    best_score = _authoritative_score(best, best_local)

    def _maybe_phase(frac, min_time, fn, phase_tag):
        pass

    # Entropy warmup / flattening.
    allowed = min(float(time_limit) * 0.16, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if allowed > 12.0:
        best, sc = _entropy_refine(best, allowed, tag + " ent1")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local:
            best_local = sc
        best_score = _authoritative_score(best, best_local)

    # Main LSE L-BFGS.
    allowed = min(float(time_limit) * 0.32, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if allowed > 18.0:
        best, sc = _lbfgs_refine(best, allowed, tag + " lb1")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local:
            best_local = sc
        best_score = _authoritative_score(best, best_local)

    # Active-set subgradient.
    allowed = min(float(time_limit) * 0.14, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if allowed > 12.0:
        best, sc = _subgradient_refine(best, allowed, tag + " sub1")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local:
            best_local = sc
        best_score = _authoritative_score(best, best_local)

    # Second LSE polishing pass.
    allowed = min(float(time_limit) * 0.14, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if allowed > 18.0:
        best, sc = _lbfgs_refine(best, allowed, tag + " lb2")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local:
            best_local = sc
        best_score = _authoritative_score(best, best_local)

    # Entropy fine tuning.
    allowed = min(float(time_limit) * 0.10, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if allowed > 10.0:
        best, sc = _entropy_refine(best, allowed, tag + " ent2")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local:
            best_local = sc
        best_score = _authoritative_score(best, best_local)

    # Final subgradient.
    allowed = min(float(time_limit) * 0.14, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if allowed > 8.0:
        best, sc = _subgradient_refine(best, allowed, tag + " sub2")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local:
            best_local = sc
        best_score = _authoritative_score(best, best_local)

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Analytic initial shapes.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _half_binomial(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= _TINY:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / s


def _initial_candidates(main_len, height_a):
    main_len = int(main_len)
    if main_len <= 0:
        half = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        return half, _score_fast(half), half

    half = _half_binomial(main_len)
    cands = []

    if height_a is not None and len(height_a) == main_len:
        cands.append(height_a)

    cands.append(half)

    if height_a is not None and len(height_a) == main_len:
        mix1 = 0.8 * height_a + 0.2 * half
        cands.append(_clip_normalize(mix1))

        mix2 = 0.5 * height_a + 0.5 * half[::-1]
        cands.append(_clip_normalize(mix2))

    # Symmetric edge-singular shape.
    i = np.arange(main_len, dtype=np.float64)
    edge = np.minimum(i, float(main_len - 1) - i)
    u = 1.0 / np.sqrt(edge + 1.0)
    cands.append(_clip_normalize(u))

    # Two-edge arcsine-like shape.
    denom = (i + 1.0) * (float(main_len) - i)
    denom = np.maximum(denom, 1e-12)
    arc = 1.0 / np.sqrt(denom)
    cands.append(_clip_normalize(arc))

    # Slightly shifted half-binomial.
    cands.append(_clip_normalize(0.9 * half + 0.1 * half[::-1]))

    # Uniform fallback.
    cands.append(np.ones(main_len, dtype=np.float64) / main_len)

    best = cands[0]
    best_sc = _score_fast(best)

    for c in cands[1:]:
        sc = _score_fast(c)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = c
            best_sc = sc

    return best, float(best_sc), half


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0

    margin = 90.0 if budget > 200.0 else max(5.0, 0.1 * budget)
    deadline = start + max(40.0, budget - margin)

    height_a = None
    hraw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hraw is not None:
        try:
            har = np.asarray(hraw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if har.size > 0:
                height_a = _clip_normalize(har)
        except Exception:
            height_a = None

    if height_a is not None:
        n = int(height_a.size)

        if n < 1024:
            main_len = 4806
            a_height = _resample_sequence(height_a, main_len)
        elif n > 8192:
            main_len = 8192
            a_height = _resample_sequence(height_a, main_len)
        else:
            main_len = n
            a_height = height_a
    else:
        main_len = 4806
        a_height = None

    if a_height is None:
        a_height = _half_binomial(main_len)

    initial, init_local, _half = _initial_candidates(main_len, a_height)
    best_a = initial
    best_score = _authoritative_score(best_a, init_local)

    print(
        f"initial: length={len(best_a)}, local={init_local:.8f}, authoritative={best_score:.8f}"
    )

    # Main refinement on the best initial sequence.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t_main = min(remaining * 0.62, 1650.0)
    if t_main > 45.0:
        best_a, sc_main = _refine_sequence(best_a, t_main, "main")
        local_main = _score_fast(best_a)
        best_score = _authoritative_score(best_a, local_main)
        print(f"after main: length={len(best_a)}, authoritative={best_score:.8f}")

    # Alternative length: often a power of two gives a different local basin.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 650.0:
        a_alt = _resample_sequence(best_a, 4096)
        t_alt = min(360.0, remaining * 0.20)
        a_alt, sc_alt = _refine_sequence(a_alt, t_alt, "alt4096")
        alt_score = _authoritative_score(a_alt, sc_alt)
        if alt_score < best_score:
            best_a = a_alt
            best_score = alt_score
            print(f"alt4096 improved: score={best_score:.8f}")

    # Independent half-binomial start on the main length.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 750.0:
        a_half = _half_binomial(main_len)
        rng_half = np.random.default_rng(555123)
        a_half = a_half * (1.0 + 0.012 * rng_half.normal(size=main_len))
        a_half = _clip_normalize(a_half)

        t_half = min(300.0, remaining * 0.15)
        a_half, sc_half = _refine_sequence(a_half, t_half, "half")
        half_score = _authoritative_score(a_half, sc_half)

        if half_score < best_score:
            best_a = a_half
            best_score = half_score
            print(f"half restart improved: score={best_score:.8f}")

    # Random kick from the current best.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 550.0:
        n = len(best_a)
        rng_kick = np.random.default_rng(777101)
        a_kick = best_a * (1.0 + 0.02 * rng_kick.normal(size=n))
        a_kick = _clip_normalize(a_kick)

        t_kick = min(220.0, remaining * 0.15)
        a_kick, sc_kick = _refine_sequence(a_kick, t_kick, "kick")
        kick_score = _authoritative_score(a_kick, sc_kick)

        if kick_score < best_score:
            best_a = a_kick
            best_score = kick_score
            print(f"kick improved: score={best_score:.8f}")

    # Final short polish on the global best.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
    if remaining > 25.0:
        t_fin = min(130.0, remaining)
        best_a, sc_fin = _subgradient_refine(best_a, t_fin, "final")
        fin_score = _authoritative_score(best_a, sc_fin)
        best_score = fin_score
        print(f"after final polish: score={best_score:.8f}")

    # Make sure the returned representation is nonnegative and normalized.
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)

    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s <= _TINY:
        best_a = _half_binomial(4806)
    else:
        best_a = best_a / s

    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    final_external = _external_score(out)
    final_local = _score_fast(out)
    print(
        f"final: length={len(out)}, local={final_local:.8f}, external={final_external:.8f}"
    )

    return out
```
